Parlay board

How the board is built These are the most confident moneylines left after Pixel’s Pick takes its legs, stacked in confidence order until the combined price reaches +100 or better. No game appears twice, so each ticket is genuinely different rather than a reshuffle of the same games. Short favourites are worth little on their own; combining them is what turns them into a payout worth the risk. It does not turn them into an edge — every leg still carries its own vig, and the tracker records how these actually land.

Why this is a Pick

Toledo Rockets is a Pick: the calibrated chance of winning is 73%. The price (-325) implies 76%, more than the model gives — the confidence is real but the book is charging for it.

Since 2023, college moneylines the model rated as Picks have gone 303-99 (75%), returning -6.4% per unit staked — a solid play at a price that already knows it.

Locked (). This is the pick of record: it will not change and it is what the tracker grades.

How the model sees it playing out

The power ratings see a clear talent gap — Toledo Rockets rates 13.1 points stronger before any situational adjustment. Overall, Akron Zips is the 130th-rated team in the league (a real weakness) and Toledo Rockets is 53rd (solid). Akron Zips then adds the home-field bump the model fits at 3.9 points. Rolling that together, the engine projects Toledo Rockets by 12.5 and gives them a 78% chance to win outright.

Projected score: Akron Zips 18, Toledo Rockets 31 against a market total of 49.5.

On how it plays out: the passing game tilts toward Toledo Rockets — a major mismatch through the air. Expect the favorite to be able to play from ahead and lean on the run late.

The market has this at Toledo Rockets -9.5. That is a real disagreement: the model wants 3.2 more points on Toledo Rockets than the market is pricing, which is where the value on this game sits.

Both sides are starting their usual quarterbacks with no notable injury gap.
